<script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        const searchFieldEl = document.getElementById("search-field");

        if (!searchFieldEl) return; 
        const regexp =  /^[\p{L}\u0900-\u097F\s]+$/u;

        searchFieldEl.addEventListener("paste", (e) => {
            const value = e.target.value;
            const clipboardData = e.clipboardData || window.clipboardData;
            const clipboardVal = clipboardData.getData("text/plain");
            
            const isValid = regexp.test(clipboardVal);
            if (!isValid)  e.preventDefault();
        });

        searchFieldEl.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
            const key = e.key;

            const isValid = regexp.test(key);
            if (!isValid)  e.preventDefault();
        })
    });
</script>

<div class="rich-text">
    <div class="component-content">
        <div class="field-text"><h2>WHY CHOOSE TRUE MATCH?</h2>

<script>
        var bvEventsConfigured = false;

        function bvSubmissionLoad(data) {
            const { contentType } = data;
            let writeReviewTag = {
                "event": "uaevent",
                "event_name": "write_review",
                "ecommerce": "undefined",
                "eventAction": "write review",
                "eventCategory": "product detail page",
                "eventLabel": "true match super-blendable skincare infused foundation for buildable medium coverage::071249671870",
                "product_info": "true match super-blendable skincare infused foundation for buildable medium coverage::071249671870"
            };
            switch (contentType) {
                case "review":
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        window.dataLayer.push(writeReviewTag);
                    }, 500);
                    break;
                default:
                    break;
            }
        }

        function reviewSubmitted(data) {
            if (data.contentType === "review") {
                setTimeout(() => {
                    window.dataLayer.push({
                        event: "uaevent",
                        event_name: "submit_review",
                        ecommerce: "undefined",
                        eventAction: "submit a review",
                        eventCategory: "product detail page",
                        eventLabel: "true match super-blendable skincare infused foundation for buildable medium coverage::071249671870",
                        product_info: "true match super-blendable skincare infused foundation for buildable medium coverage::071249671870"
                    });
                }, 500);
            }
        }

        function configureBvEvents(BV) {
            if (bvEventsConfigured || !BV || !BV.configure) {
                return;
            }
            bvEventsConfigured = true;
            BV.configure('global', {
                events: {
                    submissionLoad: function (data) {
                        bvSubmissionLoad(data);
                    },
                    submissionSubmitted: function (data) {
                        reviewSubmitted(data);
                    }
                }
            });
        }

        window.bvCallback = configureBvEvents;

        (function registerBvEventsOnLazyLoad() {
            var scriptSrc = 'https://apps.bazaarvoice.com/deployments/loreal/main_site/production/en_US/bv.js';
            var configureWhenReady = function () {
                configureBvEvents(window.BV || window.$BV);
            };

            if (window.$INJECTED_URLS && window.$INJECTED_URLS[scriptSrc]) {
                window.$INJECTED_URLS[scriptSrc].then(configureWhenReady);
                return;
            }

            var attempts = 0;
            var interval = setInterval(function () {
                attempts++;
                if (window.$INJECTED_URLS && window.$INJECTED_URLS[scriptSrc]) {
                    clearInterval(interval);
                    window.$INJECTED_URLS[scriptSrc].then(configureWhenReady);
                } else if (window.BV || window.$BV) {
                    clearInterval(interval);
                    configureWhenReady();
                } else if (attempts >= 60) {
                    clearInterval(interval);
                }
            }, 500);
        })();
</script>
